## October 14 CRM startup database-path investigation — October 20, 2026

**Result: target-path and fallback logic VERIFIED by source inspection; runtime failure details remain untested.** Reviewed the approved October 14 package source at commit `1e5c36560ecc651b26a50b40639ebe01b9afa34f`, whose application files match approved baseline `3db33ca6ad96ecf72075dc2428069c2d8918ba0c`. No executable was launched, no database or package was opened for write, and no preparation, VM/network change, deployment, or application/repository change occurred other than this handoff report.

### Exact startup path resolution

- `Form1.New` loads settings first; `Form1_Load` then calls `CRMDatabase.EnsureReady()` with no path arguments (`Form1.vb:74-106`). There is no startup command-line database override and no second startup call that selects another target.
- Target database precedence is: (1) nonblank `CRM_DATABASE_PATH`; (2) if current is absent/blank, nonblank legacy `WEEKLY_LOGGER_DATABASE_PATH`; (3) otherwise `AppContext.BaseDirectory\Data\CRM.sqlite` (`Data/CRMCompatibility.vb:14-18`; `Data/CRMPaths.vb:8-20`). `Path.GetFullPath` normalizes an override; a relative override therefore depends on the process current directory, not the application directory. Blank/whitespace current and legacy values fall through. The October 14 ZIP contains `Data\CRM.sqlite`, but the code—not ZIP presence—controls selection.
- `EnsureReady` resolves both the selected target and source paths before checking target existence (`Data/CRMDatabase.vb:12-23`). Source precedence is: (1) nonblank `CRM_SOURCE_DATABASE_PATH`; (2) legacy `WEEKLY_LOGGER_SOURCE_DATABASE_PATH`; (3) `AppContext.BaseDirectory\Database\CRM_seed.sqlite` if that file exists; (4) otherwise `AppContext.BaseDirectory\Database\WeeklyLogger_seed.sqlite`, whether or not the legacy seed exists (`Data/CRMPaths.vb:22-29`). An invalid source override can therefore fail path normalization even when the target already exists; a merely missing source file is irrelevant until target initialization is needed.
- If the selected target exists, it is used in place. The source and bundled/default target are not considered as substitutes. Startup next checks/makes a migration backup when needed, opens this same target read-write, enables foreign keys/busy timeout, sets WAL mode, validates schema, migrates through v24, and checks integrity/foreign keys (`Data/CRMDatabase.vb:33-55,100-175`).
- If the selected target does not exist and its filename equals `CRM.sqlite` (case-insensitive), CRM first checks for `WeeklyLogger.sqlite` in the *same selected directory*. If present, it creates an independent SQLite backup copy at the selected target. This applies to the default directory and to an overridden path named `CRM.sqlite` (`Data/CRMDatabase.vb:23-30`).
- Otherwise the chosen source path above is copied into the selected target. Copying uses a read-only source connection, a new `<target>.initializing` database, SQLite `BackupDatabase`, integrity/foreign-key validation, then `File.Move` to the target; any pre-existing `.initializing` file is deleted and a failed temporary copy is cleaned up (`Data/CRMDatabase.vb:59-98`). This initializes the selected target; it does not continue using the legacy/seed file.
- If neither qualifying adjacent legacy database nor chosen source is available, CRM throws `FileNotFoundException`; it does not create an empty database and does not fall back to the packaged `Data\CRM.sqlite`. If `File.Exists` reports the modern seed present, it wins over the legacy seed; if that chosen modern seed is invalid or later proves inaccessible, there is no retry with the legacy seed. If `File.Exists` reports it absent (including some access-error cases), the legacy seed path is selected instead.
- Migration scripts have no alternative search chain: startup begins with `AppContext.BaseDirectory\Database\CRM_v5.sql`, then resolves `CRM_v5.sql` through `CRM_v24.sql` from that directory. A missing or incorrectly version-stamping script throws; CRM does not select another database (`Data/CRMDatabase.vb:19-21,112-134`).

### Missing, inaccessible, or invalid target behavior

- **Missing:** CRM attempts the adjacent-legacy rule, then source-copy rule, at the already selected target path. It may create the target directory, delete a stale `.initializing` file, and create/move the new database. Failure to find/open/copy the source or create/move the target propagates as an exception; no other target is tried.
- **Inaccessible/locked/read-only:** there is no access-error fallback. If `File.Exists` reports the target present, read-only version inspection and then a read-write/WAL connection are attempted; any access/lock/write failure propagates. If Windows/.NET reports an inaccessible path as nonexistent, code follows the missing-target initialization branch and normally fails when reading the source or creating the target. The exact `File.Exists` result, SQLite error, timeout, and sidecar behavior for a particular ACL/share/lock require runtime testing.
- **Invalid content/schema:** a non-SQLite or corrupt file fails during read-only `PRAGMA user_version`, backup validation, opening, or integrity checks. A valid SQLite database below schema 4 is rejected; a database above v24 is rejected; integrity or foreign-key failures are rejected. None causes target switching. A below-v24 file can receive a timestamped adjacent migration backup before later validation fails; a newer or v24-invalid file is opened read-write and WAL mode is requested before the rejection (`Data/CRMDatabase.vb:33-39,100-110,136-175`).
- All exceptions escaping `EnsureReady` are caught by `Form1_Load`: CRM writes a diagnostic entry if possible, displays `CRM could not finish starting: <message>`, and calls `Close()` (`Form1.vb:102-128`). Source therefore proves fail-and-close rather than silent target switching. Whether the packaged UI closes cleanly in every failure mode, the exact message/provider error, and which WAL/SHM/backup/temp artifacts a particular failure leaves require controlled runtime tests and were not tested.

### Silent behavior versus errors

- Silent path selection occurs only when a higher-precedence environment value is absent/blank, when the modern seed is absent and the legacy seed path is selected, or when a missing target is initialized from the adjacent legacy/source database. These branches do not prompt the user.
- Once a nonblank target override is selected, missing/inaccessible/invalid conditions never cause CRM to switch to the app-adjacent `Data\CRM.sqlite` or another target. Initialization copies data *to the configured target*; subsequent work uses that target. Failures error out and close startup.

### Startup writes unrelated to choosing the target

- Before database resolution, `OrderFormSettings.Load` reads `CRM_SETTINGS_PATH`, then legacy `WEEKLY_LOGGER_SETTINGS_PATH`, then app-adjacent `settings.json`. Path normalization can rewrite that settings file. Legacy agreement-folder migration can create the new folder, copy/delete managed documents, and save settings. Settings read/JSON errors are silently replaced with defaults, but failures from the later agreement-folder migration can block startup before `EnsureReady` (`OrderFormSettings.vb:26-53,120-152`; `AgreementDocumentStorage.vb:65-110`).
- A startup exception writes/appends a daily log under nonblank `CRM_LOG_FOLDER`, then legacy `WEEKLY_LOGGER_LOG_FOLDER`, or `%LocalAppData%\DeltaTrak\CRM\Logs`; it creates the directory and deletes logs beyond the newest 30 (`DiagnosticLog.vb:11-45`). Logging failure is swallowed.
- After database readiness, the weekly-backup check runs immediately. Blank `BackupFolder` performs no backup; otherwise a backup older than seven days triggers directory creation, a temporary SQLite backup, final move/timestamp, and pruning beyond ten backups. Backup errors are logged and shown as a warning rather than selecting another database (`Form1.vb:106-110,993-1010`; `Data/CRMBackupService.vb:13-42,84-107`).
- Startup then calls `CRMRepository.MigrateAgreementDocuments`, which opens the selected database through the same read-write/WAL connection, begins a transaction, and may create/rename/delete agreement folders/owner markers, move documents, update `AgreementDocument.StoredRelativePath`, and clean empty folders (`Data/CRMRepository.vb:19-30,88-213`; `AgreementDocumentStorage.vb:10-55`). Subsequent initial data loads also use read-write/WAL connections even for queries. No automatic network, browser, email, export, or deployment action was found on the main-form startup path.

### Runtime-only evidence still required

No launch was authorized. A later isolated test would be needed to prove the effective current directory for relative overrides; inherited environment values in the packaged process; concrete ACL/read-only-share/locked-file behavior; exact SQLite exceptions and sidecars; failure-dialog/close behavior; and whether the actual settings, backup age, or agreement records trigger their conditional writes. Existing source/tests establish copying and migration intent but do not cover the complete missing/inaccessible/corrupt startup matrix. CRM preparation and execution remain blocked pending separate explicit authorization.

### Evidence classification

The package hash is still `73A705321CFA2858B52ABBD0409581361ADD0542EBF45393E2B6754DDE676CA0`; read-only ZIP inspection found 316 entries including `CRM.exe`, bundled `Data\CRM.sqlite`, `Database\CRM_seed.sqlite`, and migrations v5-v24, but no `settings.json`, `portable-config.json`, database sidecar, or application settings file.
- **Configuration-dependent:** effective paths depend on the exact child-process environment, absolute JSON values, launch identity/profile, whether the working DB already exists, current settings/legacy folders, backup age, agreement rows/files, and host VM attachments. The investigator's process/user/machine environment currently has none of the eight database/source/settings/log override variables set, but this does not prove the future `Dev` or test-account process environment.
- **Runtime-unverified:** the package has not been launched under proposed overrides. Environment inheritance, actual `AppContext.BaseDirectory`/working directory, absence of settings rewrites, SQLite WAL/SHM behavior, agreement migration effects, VB `SaveMySettingsOnExit` metadata, and post-isolation resource denial remain unproven.

### Supported overrides and future launch context

`CRMCompatibility.EnvironmentOverride` reads the named environment variable from the process environment and, if it is absent/blank, reads the name with `CRM_` replaced by `WEEKLY_LOGGER_` (`Data/CRMCompatibility.vb:14-18`). Values are inherited by a child process; do not use persistent `setx` or machine/user changes. A future operator should set both current and legacy names to the same absolute disposable path in one test-only parent process, verify them there, set an explicit application working directory, then—only after separate launch authorization—start the verified package's exact `CRM.exe` as that child.

| Purpose | Current then legacy variable | Source-proven default | Example future absolute value |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pre-existing working DB | `CRM_DATABASE_PATH`, `WEEKLY_LOGGER_DATABASE_PATH` | `<AppBase>\Data\CRM.sqlite` | `C:\CRM-Display-Test\run-001\working\CRM.sqlite` |
| Initialization source guard | `CRM_SOURCE_DATABASE_PATH`, `WEEKLY_LOGGER_SOURCE_DATABASE_PATH` | existing `<AppBase>\Database\CRM_seed.sqlite`, else `<AppBase>\Database\WeeklyLogger_seed.sqlite` | `C:\CRM-Display-Test\run-001\guard\MUST-NOT-EXIST.sqlite` |
| Settings JSON | `CRM_SETTINGS_PATH`, `WEEKLY_LOGGER_SETTINGS_PATH` | `<AppBase>\settings.json` | `C:\CRM-Display-Test\run-001\config\settings.json` |
| Diagnostic logs | `CRM_LOG_FOLDER`, `WEEKLY_LOGGER_LOG_FOLDER` | `%LocalAppData%\DeltaTrak\CRM\Logs` | `C:\CRM-Display-Test\run-001\logs` |

- The source guard is intentionally nonexistent inside the disposable root. The working corrected DB must already exist, and no adjacent `WeeklyLogger.sqlite` may exist. If the target disappears, startup must fail instead of cloning the bundled/default/legacy/seed database. The source path is normalized even when unused, so it must be syntactically valid and absolute.
- Use exact, absolute, non-reparse paths. The current variable wins; the legacy variable is consulted only when the current value is absent/blank. There is no command-line database/settings/log override. Relative values depend on process current directory and are unacceptable.
- The October 14 package uses `SaveMySettingsOnExit=true` but contains no `Settings.settings`; whether the VB runtime writes profile metadata is unverified. A disposable test profile remains required.

### Settings, agreement documents, and backups

- The exact JSON keys are `AgreementDocumentsFolder` and `BackupFolder`; there are no dedicated environment variables for either. Redirect the settings *file* with `CRM_SETTINGS_PATH`/legacy, then use custom absolute local values inside it. Also set custom absolute `ExcelTemplatePath` and `OutputFolder` inside the same root to avoid default/token normalization; keep export paths blank unless separately needed.
- Example future settings fragment (stage offline only under separate authorization): `{"ExcelTemplatePath":"C:\\CRM-Display-Test\\run-001\\app\\Blank Order Form.xlsx","OutputFolder":"C:\\CRM-Display-Test\\run-001\\outputs","AgreementDocumentsFolder":"C:\\CRM-Display-Test\\run-001\\agreements","BackupFolder":""}`. Preserve exact property casing and validate the complete JSON before use.
- If settings are missing, CRM constructs defaults in memory: agreements use `%LocalAppData%\DeltaTrak\CRM\Agreement Documents`, output uses `<AppBase>\Orders`, template uses `<AppBase>\Blank Order Form.xlsx`, and `BackupFolder` is blank. Malformed, unreadable, or deserialized-null settings are silently replaced with those defaults (`OrderFormSettings.vb:34-53`). Missing/malformed settings therefore do not safely isolate agreement documents.
- Blank `BackupFolder` is the safest display-test value and makes the startup weekly-backup call return without enumerating or writing a backup location. A nonblank value is normalized with `Path.GetFullPath`; when no managed backup is newer than seven days, startup creates the folder, writes `<folder>\CRM-backup-<timestamp>.sqlite` via `.creating`, timestamps it, and prunes beyond ten (`CRMBackupService.vb:13-42,84-107`). Never point this value to Z:, USB, network, preserved files, or the Dev profile.
- `OrderFormSettings.Load` can rewrite the redirected settings file when it normalizes blank/token/former deployment defaults. `MigrateDefaultAgreementFolder` then examines `%LocalAppData%\DeltaTrak\WeeklyLogger\Agreement Documents` and the current CRM default; when triggered it can copy/delete managed folders and save settings (`OrderFormSettings.vb:56-152`; `AgreementDocumentStorage.vb:65-110`). A custom absolute disposable `AgreementDocumentsFolder` avoids that source-proven migration branch; this still needs runtime confirmation.
- After DB readiness, `CRMRepository.MigrateAgreementDocuments` uses `AgreementDocumentsFolder` as `AgreementStorageRoot`, opens the working DB read-write/WAL, and may create/rename/delete folders and ownership markers, move documents, update `AgreementDocument.StoredRelativePath`, and clean empty folders (`CRMRepository.vb:88-213`). The root must be empty/disposable and local; do not copy real agreement documents.

### Startup exposure and no-code limitation

- The pre-existing working DB is opened read-write and `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL` is executed. `-wal` and `-shm` belong beside the working copy; migration/backup/initialization paths can also create timestamped DB backups, `.initializing`, `.creating`, and SQLite journal artifacts. Schema-24 preflight avoids migrations but not WAL/header/sidecar writes.
- Indirect Z:/host access can come from any database/source/settings/log environment value, the settings-file path, `AgreementDocumentsFolder`, `BackupFolder`, template/output/export values, adjacent `WeeklyLogger.sqlite`, bundled seeds when a target is missing, or an operator-selected path. Configuration must reject Z:, UNC, mapped/network/removable/USB locations, reparse points, the preserved original/corrected DBs/manifest, and the Dev profile for every path and ancestor.
- No automatic startup browser, email, Graph, export, or geocoding action was found. Network-capable commands remain available after startup. Environment/settings routing can keep all *source-proven automatic startup writes* under one disposable local root, but it cannot prevent the process from accessing Z:, the host share, Dev files, USB, or network through other code or user action. A thumb-drive or other backup does not make unmonitored writes to preserved files acceptable.

## Completed documentation — disposable CRM display-test preparation procedure (October 20, 2026)

**Status and scope:** Documentation only was completed from the established October 14 source/path findings. None of the commands below was run. No CRM process was launched; no database, package, settings file, VM/network/permission setting, application source, or production file was opened for write, copied, or changed. ### Proposed future operator procedure — requires separate preparation authorization

All paths and commands in this section are proposed future instructions. Run them only after Rob explicitly authorizes preparation. Run the whole preparation in one dedicated PowerShell session, stop on the first failed assertion, capture its transcript outside production paths, and do **not** start `CRM.exe`.

#### 1. Read-only Z: preflight

The three preserved files must exist at the exact paths, sizes, and hashes below. Hashing and metadata reads are permitted only in a future authorized preparation run; neither SQLite database is to be opened on Z:. The independent backup location is not part of this check and must not be accessed.

| Preserved file | Expected bytes | Expected SHA-256 |
|---|---:|---|
| `Z:\CRM.sqlite` | 2,195,456 | `FF4EC6C909C38171F5C45258A7147CA4EB79787F8F211D4C0B02C26A97DD7A5D` |
| `Z:\CRM_corrected.sqlite` | 2,195,456 | `B4B5D575FA9E494605F3BEBEBA9D9B5F85B82867453ED5D6816B03148BCE5B13` |
| `Z:\CRM_correction_manifest.json` | 58,389 | `8AA91C24DD1F70CF6DC737A94508BB811DDECDFAC8B2EEA942B9BFB6D3992E24` |

Proposed read-only preflight:

```powershell
$Expected = [ordered]@{
  'Z:\CRM.sqlite' = @{ Length = 2195456; Sha256 = 'FF4EC6C909C38171F5C45258A7147CA4EB79787F8F211D4C0B02C26A97DD7A5D' }
  'Z:\CRM_corrected.sqlite' = @{ Length = 2195456; Sha256 = 'B4B5D575FA9E494605F3BEBEBA9D9B5F85B82867453ED5D6816B03148BCE5B13' }
  'Z:\CRM_correction_manifest.json' = @{ Length = 58389; Sha256 = '8AA91C24DD1F70CF6DC737A94508BB811DDECDFAC8B2EEA942B9BFB6D3992E24' }
}
foreach ($Path in $Expected.Keys) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path -PathType Leaf)) { throw "Missing preserved file: $Path" }
  $Item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path
  $Hash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
  if ($Item.Length -ne $Expected[$Path].Length -or $Hash -ne $Expected[$Path].Sha256) {
    throw "Preserved file identity mismatch: $Path"
  }
  [pscustomobject]@{ Path=$Path; Bytes=$Item.Length; LastWriteTimeUtc=$Item.LastWriteTimeUtc; SHA256=$Hash }
}
$Sidecars = foreach ($Db in 'Z:\CRM.sqlite','Z:\CRM_corrected.sqlite') {
  "$Db-wal"; "$Db-shm"; "$Db-journal"
}
$PresentSidecars = @($Sidecars | Where-Object { Test-Path -LiteralPath $_ })
if ($PresentSidecars.Count -ne 0) { throw "SQLite sidecar present on Z:: $($PresentSidecars -join ', ')" }
```

Any missing file, size/hash mismatch, or `-wal`, `-shm`, or `-journal` companion is an immediate **STOP / PREPARATION NO-GO**. Record the discrepancy; do not copy, open, checkpoint, or repair anything.

#### 2. Create the disposable local layout and no-overwrite copies

## CRM contact-save collateral-write correction completed — October 20, 2026

**Outcome:** The defect was reproduced first against a fresh disposable copy of the verified corrected database, then corrected and fully regression-tested on isolated branch `codex/crm-contact-save-fix`. Project commit `d502271dd66fa5b0a9491c7ec67e54b51cdbd0c6` is pushed and verified equal to `origin/codex/crm-contact-save-fix`. The branch starts directly from approved baseline `3db33ca6ad96ecf72075dc2428069c2d8918ba0c`; unauthorized authentication commit `86a1dff` is not in its ancestry. The fix is unmerged and undeployed.

### Reproduction and root cause

- The sealed corrected reference was read only for no-overwrite copying and matched SHA-256 `B4B5D575FA9E494605F3BEBEBA9D9B5F85B82867453ED5D6816B03148BCE5B13`. Creating a synthetic contact at Site 1 on the disposable baseline reproduced every reported collateral write: CompanyAccountId 1 `IsPrimary 0 -> 1`; ContactId 1 `JobTitle '' -> NULL` and `UpdatedUtc` changed; ContactMethodId 1 `IsPrimary 0 -> 1`; SiteLocation SiteId 1 `AddressFingerprint '11360 santa fe drive, ballico, california, usa' -> NULL` and `UpdatedUtc` changed.
- `Form1.SaveChangesAsync` unconditionally called `UpdateCompany`, `UpdateSite`, `UpdateContact`, and the interaction-date writer for every displayed contact whenever any site/contact data was saved. The address decision compared a newly formatted UI address to a differently composed stored display address. Repository helpers then reselected unchanged primary account/contact-method rows, normalized empty strings to NULL, refreshed timestamps, and invalidated the site fingerprint.

### Exact correction

- `Form1.vb` now compares persisted company, site, address-component, contact, and interaction-date fields and calls only the writers whose data changed. Added/deleted contacts and deliberate site/activity changes retain their existing transactional workflow.
- `Data/CRMRepository.vb` now calls primary-account selection only when the customer number actually changes; an unchanged normalized contact method is not reselected; and unchanged empty/NULL contact text preserves its exact stored representation during a different intentional contact edit.
- `tests/CRM.Ui.Tests/ContactSaveRegressionTests.cs` covers disposable corrected-copy reproduction/regression, contact create/edit/save/reload/reopen persistence, full user-table comparison, and intentional company/customer-account, site-note, address/fingerprint, and contact-email-primary behavior.

### Verification and limits

- Corrected-copy tests passed 2/2. The create/edit/reopen test compared every user table before/after: every pre-existing row was identical, with only the intended one Contact, one ContactMethod, one SiteContact, and two Activity audit rows added. The intentional-change test confirmed a changed customer number becomes the primary account, a site-note save preserves SiteLocation fingerprint/timestamp, an address change invalidates the fingerprint and advances its timestamp, and a changed email becomes primary without changing ContactId 1's stored title. The same tests passed against the portable migrated seed.
- Full CRM UI tests passed 89/89; full CRM data tests passed 61/61; Release solution build passed with zero warnings/errors; `git diff --check` passed. SiteContact `IsPrimary` has no contact-edit control in this baseline, so the intentional-primary check covers the affected `ContactMethod.IsPrimary` path.
- Final read-only hashes are unchanged: local corrected reference and `Z:\CRM_corrected.sqlite` are 2,195,456 bytes at `B4B5D575FA9E494605F3BEBEBA9D9B5F85B82867453ED5D6816B03148BCE5B13`; `Z:\CRM.sqlite` is 2,195,456 bytes at `FF4EC6C909C38171F5C45258A7147CA4EB79787F8F211D4C0B02C26A97DD7A5D`. No Z-drive SQLite sidecar was present. Disposable temp databases were removed.
- No production/corrected-master database, schema, email, external order, package, deployment, branch merge, or production switch-over occurred. Stop here for Rob's review; any merge, packaging, manual executable test, corrected-master replacement, deployment, or production change requires separate authorization.
